W15.8.1.2-R10R3 � CANONICAL ANNOUNCEMENT API IMPLEMENTATION PLAN & MIGRATION BOUNDARY
Repository: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon
Generated: 2026-09-14 09:35:29
INSPECTION ONLY � NO SOURCE CHANGES AUTHORIZED

====================================================================
OBJECTIVE
====================================================================
Freeze the smallest safe implementation boundary for:

Operations Center announcement UI
    -> canonical production Announcement API
    -> authenticated Principal
    -> AuthorizationService
    -> Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH
    -> Publication draft / approval lifecycle
    -> APPROVED
    -> ApprovedPublicationExecutionService
    -> DurablePublicationExecutionService
    -> ControlledAnnouncementPublisher
    -> Telegram
    -> audit

Announcement scope MUST remain:
- Authentication
- AuthorizationService
- Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH
- Existing approval workflow
- Auditability
- Controlled Telegram publication

Explicitly OUT OF SCOPE:
- MFA
- WebAuthn/passkey step-up
- W15.6 authentication freshness requirement
- W15.7 transaction authorization
- new authentication provider
- new Telegram provider
- new persistence architecture
- unrelated refactoring
- replacement of unrelated working functionality

This gate must identify:
- one authoritative production mutation path;
- exact API/router boundary;
- exact application services to reuse;
- exact persistence composition;
- exact authorization dependency;
- actor provenance;
- legacy AdminAuthorizer migration/isolation;
- exact implementation allowlist;
- exact regression-test allowlist;
- exact files that must remain untouched;
- exact 4-eyes approval conditions.

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/api/dependencies/identity.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, status

from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal
from app.infrastructure.auth.session_identity_provider import (
    SessionIdentityProvider,
)


async def get_current_principal(request: Request) -> Principal:
    """Resolve identity from the HttpOnly application session cookie."""

    session_id = request.cookies.get("easychatbot_session")

    repository = request.app.state.auth_repository

    provider = SessionIdentityProvider(
        session_id=session_id,
        repository=repository,
    )

    principal = await provider.get_current_principal()

    if principal is None or not principal.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Authentication required.",
        )

    return principal

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/api/dependencies/authorization.py

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status

from app.api.dependencies.identity import get_current_principal
from app.application.authorization.authorization_service import (
    AuthorizationService,
    PermissionDeniedError,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


def require_permission(permission: Permission):
    async def dependency(
        principal: Principal = Depends(get_current_principal),
    ) -> Principal:
        try:
            return AuthorizationService().require_permission(
                principal=principal,
                permission=permission,
            )
        except PermissionDeniedError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

    return dependency

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/authorization/authorization_service.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission, has_permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


class AuthenticationRequiredError(PermissionError):
    """No valid authenticated principal exists."""


class PermissionDeniedError(PermissionError):
    """Authenticated principal lacks a permission."""


class AuthorizationService:
    """Provider-neutral authorization boundary."""

    def require_authenticated(
        self,
        principal: Principal | None,
    ) -> Principal:
        if principal is None or not principal.is_active:
            raise AuthenticationRequiredError(
                "Authenticated application identity is required."
            )
        return principal

    def require_permission(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal | None,
        permission: Permission,
    ) -> Principal:
        authenticated = self.require_authenticated(principal)

        if not has_permission(
            roles=authenticated.roles,
            permission=permission,
        ):
            raise PermissionDeniedError(
                f"Permission required: {permission.value}."
            )

        return authenticated





####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/domain/authorization/permissions.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from enum import StrEnum


class Permission(StrEnum):
    ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW = "announcement.view"
    ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH = "announcement.publish"
    FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL = "functionality.control"
    INTERNAL_DATA_READ = "internal_data.read"


class Role(StrEnum):
    BETA_USER = "beta_user"
    OPERATOR = "operator"
    ADMIN = "admin"


ROLE_PERMISSIONS: dict[Role, frozenset[Permission]] = {
    Role.BETA_USER: frozenset(),
    Role.OPERATOR: frozenset(
        {
            Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW,
            Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH,
            Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL,
            Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
        }
    ),
    Role.ADMIN: frozenset(
        {
            Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW,
            Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH,
            Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL,
            Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
        }
    ),
}


def has_permission(
    *,
    roles: frozenset[str],
    permission: Permission,
) -> bool:
    return any(
        permission in ROLE_PERMISSIONS.get(
            Role(raw_role),
            frozenset(),
        )
        for raw_role in roles
        if raw_role in {role.value for role in Role}
    )

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/interfaces/announcements/template_editor_router.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import asdict
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from app.application.announcements.template_editor_service import (
    AnnouncementTemplateEditorService,
)


class PreviewRequest(BaseModel):
    template_id: str
    template_version: int | None = None
    values: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)


class CreateDraftRequest(BaseModel):
    publication_id: str | None = None
    template_id: str
    template_version: int | None = None
    title: str
    values: dict[str, str] = Field(default_factory=dict)


def create_template_editor_router(
    service: AnnouncementTemplateEditorService,
) -> APIRouter:
    """Create the HTTP adapter for the template-driven editor.

    Composition owns the service. This router never creates repositories,
    providers, Telegram clients, or publication executors.
    """

    if service is None:
        raise ValueError("service cannot be None.")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/announcement-operations/template-editor",
        tags=["announcement-operations"],
    )

    @router.get("/templates")
    def list_templates() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return [asdict(item) for item in service.list_approved_templates()]

    @router.post("/preview")
    def preview(request: PreviewRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            result = service.preview(
                template_id=request.template_id,
                values=request.values,
                version=request.template_version,
            )
        except (LookupError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc

        return asdict(result)

    @router.post("/drafts")
    def create_draft(request: CreateDraftRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            publication = service.create_draft(
                publication_id=request.publication_id or str(uuid4()),
                template_id=request.template_id,
                title=request.title,
                values=request.values,
                version=request.template_version,
            )
        except (LookupError, ValueError) as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(exc)) from exc

        return {
            "id": publication.publication_id,
            "title": publication.title,
            "content": publication.content,
            "template_id": publication.template_id,
            "template_version": publication.template_version,
            "publication_type": publication.publication_type.value,
            "status": publication.status.value,
            "created_at": publication.created_at.isoformat()
            if publication.created_at
            else None,
        }

    return router


####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/api/announcement_operations_approval.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from app.application.announcements.approval.async_approval_service import (
    AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
)


AdminAuthorizer = Callable[[], Awaitable[bool]]


class PublicationApprovalRequest(BaseModel):
    actor: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=255)


class PublicationApprovalResponse(BaseModel):
    publication_id: str
    status: str
    approved_at: str | None


async def _default_admin_authorizer() -> bool:
    return False


def create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
    *,
    approval_service: AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
    admin_authorizer: AdminAuthorizer = _default_admin_authorizer,
) -> APIRouter:
    if approval_service is None:
        raise ValueError("approval_service cannot be None.")
    if admin_authorizer is None:
        raise ValueError("admin_authorizer cannot be None.")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/announcement-operations",
        tags=["announcement-operations"],
    )

    async def require_admin() -> None:
        try:
            authorized = await admin_authorizer()
        except Exception:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Administrator authorization required.",
            ) from None

        if not authorized:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Administrator authorization required.",
            )

    @router.post(
        "/publications/{publication_id}/approve",
        response_model=PublicationApprovalResponse,
        status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        dependencies=[Depends(require_admin)],
    )
    async def approve_publication(
        publication_id: str,
        request: PublicationApprovalRequest,
    ) -> PublicationApprovalResponse:
        try:
            result = await approval_service.approve(
                publication_id=publication_id,
                actor=request.actor,
            )
        except LookupError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

        publication = result.publication
        return PublicationApprovalResponse(
            publication_id=publication.publication_id,
            status=publication.status.value,
            approved_at=(
                publication.approved_at.isoformat()
                if publication.approved_at is not None
                else None
            ),
        )

    return router


__all__ = [
    "AdminAuthorizer",
    "PublicationApprovalRequest",
    "PublicationApprovalResponse",
    "create_announcement_operations_approval_router",
]


####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/api/announcement_controls.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Awaitable, Callable
from os import getenv

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from app.application.announcement.announcement_composer import (
    AnnouncementComposer,
)
from app.application.announcement.announcement_publisher import (
    ControlledAnnouncementPublisher,
    PublicationDisabledError,
)
from app.application.announcement.models import AnnouncementType


class AnnouncementPublishRequest(BaseModel):
    """Administrator request for publishing a Beta announcement."""

    announcement_type: AnnouncementType
    title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=120)
    body: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=1800)
    footer: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=300)


class AnnouncementPublishResponse(BaseModel):
    """Controlled publication result."""

    channel_id: str
    message_id: str
    announcement_type: str
    published_at: str


AdminAuthorizer = Callable[[], Awaitable[bool]]


async def _default_admin_authorizer() -> bool:
    """
    Fail-closed default administrator authorization.

    Production composition must inject the real administrator
    authorization mechanism.
    """
    return False


def create_announcement_router(
    *,
    publisher: ControlledAnnouncementPublisher,
    composer: AnnouncementComposer | None = None,
    admin_authorizer: AdminAuthorizer = _default_admin_authorizer,
) -> APIRouter:
    """
    Create the WS-02 administrator announcement router.

    The API layer does not:
    - talk directly to Telegram;
    - construct Telegram clients;
    - bypass AnnouncementPolicy;
    - bypass ControlledAnnouncementPublisher;
    - perform autonomous publication.
    """

    if publisher is None:
        raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

    if admin_authorizer is None:
        raise ValueError("admin_authorizer cannot be None.")

    announcement_composer = composer or AnnouncementComposer()

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/admin/announcements",
        tags=["admin-announcements"],
    )

    async def require_admin() -> None:
        try:
            authorized = await admin_authorizer()
        except Exception:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Administrator authorization required.",
            ) from None

        if not authorized:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Administrator authorization required.",
            )

    @router.post(
        "/publish",
        response_model=AnnouncementPublishResponse,
        status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
        dependencies=[Depends(require_admin)],
    )
    async def publish_announcement(
        request: AnnouncementPublishRequest,
    ) -> AnnouncementPublishResponse:
        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Composition / policy validation
        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        try:
            composed = announcement_composer.compose(
                announcement_type=request.announcement_type,
                title=request.title,
                body=request.body,
                footer=request.footer,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Configuration
        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        try:
            channel_id = _resolve_channel_id()
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Controlled publication
        #
        # PublicationDisabledError is an expected operational state.
        # It is translated here at the HTTP boundary into 503.
        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        try:
            record = await publisher.publish(
                channel_id=channel_id,
                text=composed.rendered_text,
                announcement_type=(
                    composed.announcement.announcement_type.value
                ),
            )
        except PublicationDisabledError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail="Telegram announcement publishing is disabled.",
            ) from None
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        # Successful publication
        # ---------------------------------------------------------
        return AnnouncementPublishResponse(
            channel_id=record.channel_id,
            message_id=record.message_id,
            announcement_type=record.announcement_type,
            published_at=record.published_at.isoformat(),
        )

    return router


def _resolve_channel_id() -> str:
    """
    Resolve the Beta 1 announcement channel from configuration.

    The channel ID is deliberately not hard-coded.
    """

    channel_id = getenv(
        "BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID",
    )

    if not channel_id:
        raise ValueError(
            "BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID is not configured."
        )

    return channel_id


__all__ = [
    "AnnouncementPublishRequest",
    "AnnouncementPublishResponse",
    "create_announcement_router",
]

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/publication_service.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Mapping

from app.application.announcements.repositories import PublicationRepository
from app.application.announcements.template_registry import AnnouncementTemplateRegistry
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
from app.domain.announcements.template_models import PublicationStatus


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PublicationPreview:
    publication_type: str
    template_id: str
    template_version: int
    content: str


class AnnouncementPublicationService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        templates: AnnouncementTemplateRegistry,
        publications: PublicationRepository,
    ) -> None:
        if templates is None or publications is None:
            raise ValueError("templates and publications are required.")
        self._templates = templates
        self._publications = publications

    def preview(
        self,
        *,
        template_id: str,
        values: Mapping[str, str],
        version: int | None = None,
    ) -> PublicationPreview:
        template = self._templates.get_version(template_id, version)
        content = self._templates.preview(
            template_id=template_id,
            values=dict(values),
            version=template.version,
        )
        return PublicationPreview(
            publication_type=template.publication_type.value,
            template_id=template.template_id,
            template_version=template.version,
            content=content,
        )

    def create(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: str,
        template_id: str,
        title: str,
        values: Mapping[str, str],
        version: int | None = None,
    ) -> Publication:
        template = self._templates.get_version(template_id, version)
        content = self._templates.preview(
            template_id=template_id,
            values=dict(values),
            version=template.version,
        )
        publication = Publication(
            publication_id=publication_id,
            template_id=template.template_id,
            template_version=template.version,
            publication_type=template.publication_type,
            title=title,
            content=content,
            status=PublicationStatus.DRAFT,
        )
        self._publications.save(publication)
        return publication

    def _get(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
        publication = self._publications.get(publication_id)
        if publication is None:
            raise LookupError(f"Unknown publication '{publication_id}'.")
        return publication

    def request_approval(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
        publication = self._get(publication_id)
        publication.request_approval()
        self._publications.save(publication)
        return publication

    def approve(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
        publication = self._get(publication_id)
        publication.approve()
        self._publications.save(publication)
        return publication

    def begin_publish(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
        publication = self._get(publication_id)
        publication.begin_publish()
        self._publications.save(publication)
        return publication

    def mark_publish_failure(self, publication_id: str, reason: str) -> Publication:
        publication = self._get(publication_id)
        publication.mark_failure(reason)
        self._publications.save(publication)
        return publication

    def mark_publish_success(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication:
        publication = self._get(publication_id)
        publication.publish_success()
        self._publications.save(publication)
        return publication


####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/domain/announcements/publication.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from .template_models import PublicationStatus, PublicationType


_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS = {
    PublicationStatus.DRAFT: {
        PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL,
        PublicationStatus.CANCELLED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL: {
        PublicationStatus.APPROVED,
        PublicationStatus.CANCELLED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.APPROVED: {
        PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING,
        PublicationStatus.CANCELLED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING: {
        PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED,
        PublicationStatus.FAILED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.FAILED: {
        PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING,
        PublicationStatus.CANCELLED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED: set(),
    PublicationStatus.CANCELLED: set(),
}


@dataclass
class Publication:
    publication_id: str
    template_id: str
    template_version: int
    publication_type: PublicationType
    title: str
    content: str
    status: PublicationStatus = PublicationStatus.DRAFT
    created_at: datetime | None = None
    approved_at: datetime | None = None
    published_at: datetime | None = None
    failure_reason: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.publication_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("publication_id cannot be empty.")
        if not self.template_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("template_id cannot be empty.")
        if self.template_version < 1:
            raise ValueError("template_version must be >= 1.")
        if not self.title.strip():
            raise ValueError("title cannot be empty.")
        if not self.content.strip():
            raise ValueError("content cannot be empty.")
        if self.created_at is None:
            self.created_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    def transition(self, target: PublicationStatus) -> None:
        if target not in _ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[self.status]:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid publication transition: "
                f"{self.status.value} -> {target.value}"
            )
        self.status = target
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if target is PublicationStatus.APPROVED:
            self.approved_at = now
        elif target is PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED:
            self.published_at = now
            self.failure_reason = None

    def request_approval(self) -> None:
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL)

    def approve(self) -> None:
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.APPROVED)

    def begin_publish(self) -> None:
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING)

    def mark_failure(self, reason: str) -> None:
        if not reason.strip():
            raise ValueError("failure reason cannot be empty.")
        if self.status is not PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING:
            raise ValueError("A publication can only fail while publishing.")
        self.failure_reason = reason.strip()
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.FAILED)

    def publish_success(self) -> None:
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED)

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/domain/announcements/template_models.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from typing import Mapping


class PublicationType(str, Enum):
    NEW_FEATURE = "new_feature"
    MAINTENANCE = "maintenance"
    KNOWN_ISSUE = "known_issue"
    INCIDENT = "incident"
    PLANNED_DOWNTIME = "planned_downtime"
    IMPORTANT = "important"
    BETA_TEST = "beta_test"
    PRIVACY = "privacy"
    POLICY = "policy"
    BETA_UPDATE = "beta_update"


class PublicationStatus(str, Enum):
    DRAFT = "draft"
    PENDING_APPROVAL = "pending_approval"
    APPROVED = "approved"
    PUBLISHING = "publishing"
    PUBLISHED = "published"
    FAILED = "failed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AnnouncementTemplateVersion:
    template_id: str
    version: int
    publication_type: PublicationType
    structure: tuple[str, ...]
    required_fields: frozenset[str] = frozenset()
    optional_fields: frozenset[str] = frozenset()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.template_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("template_id cannot be empty.")
        if self.version < 1:
            raise ValueError("version must be >= 1.")
        if not self.structure:
            raise ValueError("structure cannot be empty.")

        declared = set(self.required_fields) | set(self.optional_fields)
        for token in self.structure:
            if token.startswith("{{") and token.endswith("}}"):
                name = token[2:-2].strip()
                if name and name not in declared:
                    raise ValueError(f"Template field '{name}' is not declared.")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AnnouncementTemplate:
    template_id: str
    name: str
    publication_type: PublicationType
    active_version: int
    versions: tuple[AnnouncementTemplateVersion, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)
    description: str = ""
    category: str | None = None

    def get_version(self, version: int | None = None) -> AnnouncementTemplateVersion:
        target = self.active_version if version is None else version
        for item in self.versions:
            if item.version == target:
                return item
        raise ValueError(
            f"Template '{self.template_id}' version {target} does not exist."
        )


def render_template(
    template: AnnouncementTemplateVersion,
    values: Mapping[str, str],
) -> str:
    missing = sorted(
        name
        for name in template.required_fields
        if not values.get(name, "").strip()
    )
    if missing:
        raise ValueError("Missing required template fields: " + ", ".join(missing))

    rendered: list[str] = []
    for token in template.structure:
        if token.startswith("{{") and token.endswith("}}"):
            name = token[2:-2].strip()
            value = values.get(name, "")
            if value:
                rendered.append(value.strip())
        else:
            rendered.append(token)

    return "\n".join(part for part in rendered if part).strip()

####################################################################

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Protocol

from app.application.announcements.publication_service import (
    AnnouncementPublicationService,
)
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication


class PublicationApprovalAudit(Protocol):
    async def record_approval(
        self,
        *,
        publication: Publication,
        actor: str,
        occurred_at: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PublicationApprovalResult:
    publication: Publication
    actor: str
    approved_at: datetime


class AnnouncementPublicationApprovalService:
    """Authorized application boundary for WS-23.1.3 approval.

    Lifecycle validation remains in the Publication domain model.
    Persistence remains behind PublicationRepository.
    Telegram delivery is deliberately outside this service.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publications: AnnouncementPublicationService,
        audit: PublicationApprovalAudit,
    ) -> None:
        if publications is None or audit is None:
            raise ValueError("publications and audit are required.")
        self._publications = publications
        self._audit = audit

    async def approve(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: str,
        actor: str,
    ) -> PublicationApprovalResult:
        if not actor.strip():
            raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")

        publication = self._publications.approve(publication_id)
        approved_at = publication.approved_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        await self._audit.record_approval(
            publication=publication,
            actor=actor.strip(),
            occurred_at=approved_at,
        )

        return PublicationApprovalResult(
            publication=publication,
            actor=actor.strip(),
            approved_at=approved_at,
        )


####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/approval/async_approval_service.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Protocol

from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
from app.domain.announcements.template_models import PublicationStatus


class AsyncPublicationStore(Protocol):
    async def get_async(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        ...

    async def save_async(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        ...


class PublicationApprovalAudit(Protocol):
    async def record_approval(
        self,
        *,
        publication: Publication,
        actor: str,
        occurred_at: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AsyncPublicationApprovalResult:
    publication: Publication
    actor: str
    approved_at: datetime


class AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService:
    """Async PostgreSQL-backed approval application boundary.

    The domain model remains the authority for lifecycle transitions.
    This service never invokes WS-22 execution or Telegram.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publications: AsyncPublicationStore,
        audit: PublicationApprovalAudit,
    ) -> None:
        if publications is None or audit is None:
            raise ValueError("publications and audit are required.")
        self._publications = publications
        self._audit = audit

    async def approve(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: str,
        actor: str,
    ) -> AsyncPublicationApprovalResult:
        if not actor.strip():
            raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")

        publication = await self._publications.get_async(publication_id)
        if publication is None:
            raise LookupError(f"Unknown publication '{publication_id}'.")

        if publication.status is not PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL:
            # Use the domain transition so the canonical lifecycle error remains
            # authoritative and consistent with the existing domain model.
            publication.approve()

        else:
            publication.approve()

        approved_at = publication.approved_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        await self._publications.save_async(publication)
        await self._audit.record_approval(
            publication=publication,
            actor=actor.strip(),
            occurred_at=approved_at,
        )

        return AsyncPublicationApprovalResult(
            publication=publication,
            actor=actor.strip(),
            approved_at=approved_at,
        )


__all__ = [
    "AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService",
    "AsyncPublicationApprovalResult",
]


####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/approval/composition.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.api.announcement_operations_approval import (
    AdminAuthorizer,
    create_announcement_operations_approval_router,
)
from app.application.announcements.approval.approval_service import (
    AnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AnnouncementApprovalComposition:
    """Composition boundary for WS-23.1.3 approval."""

    service: AnnouncementPublicationApprovalService
    router: object


def create_announcement_approval_composition(
    *,
    approval_service: AnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
    admin_authorizer: AdminAuthorizer,
) -> AnnouncementApprovalComposition:
    """
    Compose the WS-23.1.3 approval boundary.

    The composition root owns dependency creation.

    This function:
    - reuses the injected approval service;
    - reuses the injected administrator authorizer;
    - creates only the API router;
    - does not create a repository;
    - does not create a database/session;
    - does not create a Telegram client;
    - does not invoke WS-22 execution.
    """

    if approval_service is None:
        raise ValueError("approval_service cannot be None.")

    if admin_authorizer is None:
        raise ValueError("admin_authorizer cannot be None.")

    router = create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
        approval_service=approval_service,
        admin_authorizer=admin_authorizer,
    )

    return AnnouncementApprovalComposition(
        service=approval_service,
        router=router,
    )


__all__ = [
    "AnnouncementApprovalComposition",
    "create_announcement_approval_composition",
]

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/announcements/execution/durable_execution_composition.py
####################################################################
"""WS-22.3.5.3 canonical durable publication execution composition.

This module is the composition boundary for the WS-22 durable execution
vertical slice.

It deliberately does not create a new persistence architecture. The
canonical PostgreSQL engine and async session factory are supplied by the
platform persistence boundary in:

    app.infrastructure.persistence.database

The resulting object graph is:

    PostgreSQL
        |
        v
    canonical async session factory
        |
        +--> execution repository
        |
        +--> audit repository
                  |
                  v
        DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
                  |
                  v
        DurablePublicationExecutionService

Provider selection remains outside this module.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.announcements.execution.audit_adapter import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionAudit,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.publication_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Fully composed WS-22 durable execution boundary."""

    engine: AsyncEngine
    session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]
    execution_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository
    audit_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository
    audit: DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
    service: DurablePublicationExecutionService

    async def dispose(self) -> None:
        """Release the shared PostgreSQL async engine."""

        await self.engine.dispose()


def create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
    *,
    publisher,
) -> DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Create the canonical WS-22 durable execution graph.

    The publisher is deliberately injected because provider selection belongs
    outside the durable execution composition.

    This function creates exactly one PostgreSQL engine and exactly one async
    session factory. Both durable repositories receive that same factory.
    """

    if publisher is None:
        raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    session_factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)

    execution_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    audit_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    audit = DurablePublicationExecutionAudit(
        repository=audit_repository,
    )

    service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
        publisher=publisher,
        execution_repository=execution_repository,
        audit=audit,
    )

    return DurablePublicationExecutionComposition(
        engine=engine,
        session_factory=session_factory,
        execution_repository=execution_repository,
        audit_repository=audit_repository,
        audit=audit,
        service=service,
    )


__all__ = [
    "DurablePublicationExecutionComposition",
    "create_durable_publication_execution_composition",
]

13. CONTROLLED TELEGRAM BOUNDARY

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/application/announcement/announcement_publisher.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Protocol


class AnnouncementPublisher(Protocol):
    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        channel_id: str,
        text: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Publish an announcement and return its external message ID."""


class PublicationDisabledError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when controlled announcement publishing is disabled."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PublicationRecord:
    channel_id: str
    message_id: str
    announcement_type: str
    published_at: datetime


class ControlledAnnouncementPublisher:
    """Application boundary enforcing the explicit Beta 1 publication switch."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publisher: AnnouncementPublisher,
        publishing_enabled: bool,
    ) -> None:
        if publisher is None:
            raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

        self._publisher = publisher
        self._publishing_enabled = publishing_enabled

    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        channel_id: str,
        text: str,
        announcement_type: str,
    ) -> PublicationRecord:
        if not self._publishing_enabled:
            raise PublicationDisabledError(
                "Telegram announcement publishing is disabled."
            )

        message_id = await self._publisher.publish(
            channel_id=channel_id,
            text=text,
        )

        return PublicationRecord(
            channel_id=channel_id,
            message_id=message_id,
            announcement_type=announcement_type,
            published_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/infrastructure/announcements/execution/telegram_publication_publisher_adapter.py
####################################################################
"""
WS-23.1.4 Telegram publication adapter.

Architecture:

WS-22 AnnouncementPublisher
        ↓
TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter
        ↓
ControlledAnnouncementPublisher
        ↓
TelegramAnnouncementPublisher
        ↓
Existing Telegram client

This adapter:
- does not create a Telegram client;
- does not implement idempotency;
- does not create a repository;
- does not create a database;
- does not bypass the controlled publisher;
- translates the provider-independent WS-22 contract into the
  existing controlled Telegram publication boundary.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from os import getenv
from uuid import UUID

from app.application.announcement.announcement_publisher import (
    ControlledAnnouncementPublisher,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    AnnouncementPublisher,
)


class TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter(AnnouncementPublisher):
    """Bridge the canonical WS-22 publisher contract to Telegram."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publisher: ControlledAnnouncementPublisher,
        channel_id: str | None = None,
        announcement_type: str = "beta_update",
    ) -> None:
        if publisher is None:
            raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

        resolved_channel_id = channel_id or getenv(
            "BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID"
        )

        if not resolved_channel_id or not resolved_channel_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "BETA1_ANNOUNCEMENT_CHANNEL_ID is not configured."
            )

        if not announcement_type.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "announcement_type cannot be empty."
            )

        self._publisher = publisher
        self._channel_id = resolved_channel_id.strip()
        self._announcement_type = announcement_type.strip()

    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: UUID,
        title: str,
        content: str,
        correlation_id: str,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> str | None:
        """
        Publish through the existing controlled Telegram boundary.

        WS-22 remains responsible for:
        - idempotency;
        - execution persistence;
        - execution audit;
        - retry semantics.

        This adapter only translates the publication request.
        """

        if not isinstance(publication_id, UUID):
            raise ValueError(
                "publication_id must be a UUID."
            )

        if not title.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "title cannot be empty."
            )

        if not content.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "content cannot be empty."
            )

        if not correlation_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "correlation_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not idempotency_key.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "idempotency_key cannot be empty."
            )

        text = (
            f"*{title.strip()}*\n\n"
            f"{content.strip()}"
        )

        record = await self._publisher.publish(
            channel_id=self._channel_id,
            text=text,
            announcement_type=self._announcement_type,
        )

        return record.message_id


__all__ = [
    "TelegramPublicationPublisherAdapter",
]

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/infrastructure/telegram/telegram_announcement_publisher.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any


class TelegramAnnouncementPublisher:
    """Thin infrastructure adapter for the Telegram announcement channel."""

    def __init__(self, *, telegram_client: Any) -> None:
        if telegram_client is None:
            raise ValueError("telegram_client cannot be None.")

        self._telegram_client = telegram_client

    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        channel_id: str,
        text: str,
    ) -> str:
        if not channel_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("channel_id is required.")

        if not text.strip():
            raise ValueError("text is required.")

        result = await self._telegram_client.send_message(
            chat_id=channel_id,
            text=text,
            parse_mode="Markdown",
        )

        message_id = getattr(result, "message_id", None)
        if message_id is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Telegram publisher did not receive a message identifier."
            )

        return str(message_id)

####################################################################
FILE: backend/app/infrastructure/telegram/telegram_announcement_client.py
####################################################################
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from typing import Any

from telegram import Bot


class TelegramAnnouncementClient:
    """
    Outbound-only Telegram client for controlled announcements.

    This client is completely separate from TelegramBotRuntime.

    Responsibilities:
    - resolve TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN;
    - create an outbound Telegram Bot client lazily;
    - send controlled announcement messages;
    - release client resources.

    This client does NOT:
    - poll Telegram updates;
    - register handlers;
    - start polling;
    - execute classroom conversations;
    - perform administrator authorization;
    - bypass ControlledAnnouncementPublisher.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        token: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._token = (
            token
            or os.getenv("TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN")
        )

        if not self._token:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN is missing."
            )

        self._bot: Bot | None = None

    def _get_bot(self) -> Bot:
        """
        Return the outbound-only Telegram Bot client.

        The Bot is created lazily and never starts polling.
        """

        if self._bot is None:
            self._bot = Bot(
                token=self._token,
            )

        return self._bot

    async def send_message(
        self,
        *,
        chat_id: str,
        text: str,
        parse_mode: str | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        """
        Send one outbound Telegram message.

        No Telegram polling is performed.
        """

        bot = self._get_bot()

        return await bot.send_message(
            chat_id=chat_id,
            text=text,
            parse_mode=parse_mode,
        )

    async def close(self) -> None:
        """
        Release the outbound Telegram client.
        """

        if self._bot is None:
            return

        await self._bot.shutdown()

        self._bot = None


__all__ = [
    "TelegramAnnouncementClient",
]

####################################################################
FILE: frontend/app/components/operations-center/announcement-operations/TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor.tsx
####################################################################
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  createTemplateDraft,
  getEditorTemplates,
  previewTemplate,
  type TemplateEditorOption,
} from "@/app/lib/api/announcementTemplateEditor";

export default function TemplateDrivenPublicationEditor() {
  const [templates, setTemplates] = useState<TemplateEditorOption[]>([]);
  const [templateId, setTemplateId] = useState("");
  const [values, setValues] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState("");
  const [loadingTemplates, setLoadingTemplates] = useState(true);
  const [previewing, setPreviewing] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const selectedTemplate = useMemo(
    () => templates.find((item) => item.template_id === templateId) ?? null,
    [templateId, templates],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    getEditorTemplates()
      .then((items) => {
        setTemplates(items);
        if (items.length > 0) {
          setTemplateId(items[0].template_id);
        }
      })
      .catch((reason: unknown) => {
        setError(
          reason instanceof Error
            ? reason.message
            : "Announcement types could not be loaded.",
        );
      })
      .finally(() => setLoadingTemplates(false));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedTemplate) {
      setValues({});
      setPreview("");
      return;
    }

    const nextValues: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const field of selectedTemplate.fields) {
      nextValues[field.name] = "";
    }

    setValues(nextValues);
    setPreview("");
    setError(null);
  }, [selectedTemplate]);

  const requiredFieldsMissing = selectedTemplate
    ? selectedTemplate.fields.some(
        (field) => field.required && !values[field.name]?.trim(),
      )
    : true;

  const canPreview =
    Boolean(selectedTemplate) &&
    Boolean(title.trim()) &&
    !requiredFieldsMissing;

  function updateValue(name: string, value: string) {
    setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [name]: value }));
    setMessage(null);
  }

  async function handlePreview() {
    if (!selectedTemplate || !canPreview) {
      return;
    }

    setPreviewing(true);
    setError(null);
    setMessage(null);

    try {
      const result = await previewTemplate({
        templateId: selectedTemplate.template_id,
        templateVersion: selectedTemplate.version,
        values,
      });
      setPreview(result.content);
    } catch (reason: unknown) {
      setError(
        reason instanceof Error
          ? reason.message
          : "Preview could not be generated.",
      );
    } finally {
      setPreviewing(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleSaveDraft() {
    if (!selectedTemplate || !canPreview) {
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);
    setMessage(null);

    try {
      const result = await createTemplateDraft({
        templateId: selectedTemplate.template_id,
        templateVersion: selectedTemplate.version,
        title: title.trim(),
        values,
      });

      setMessage(
        `Draft ${result.id} saved from ${result.template_id} v${result.template_version}.`,
      );
    } catch (reason: unknown) {
      setError(
        reason instanceof Error
          ? reason.message
          : "The draft could not be saved.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-full p-6 lg:p-10">
      <div className="mb-8">
        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-fuchsia-300">
          Announcement Operations
        </p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-white">
          Create publication
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
          Select an approved announcement type, complete its controlled fields,
          preview it, and save a draft.
        </p>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="mb-6 rounded-2xl border border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-rose-200"
        >
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {message && (
        <div className="mb-6 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 p-4 text-sm text-emerald-200">
          {message}
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="grid gap-6 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-6">
          <h2 className="font-semibold text-white">Publication content</h2>

          <label
            htmlFor="announcement-type"
            className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
          >
            Announcement type
          </label>

          <select
            id="announcement-type"
            aria-label="Announcement type"
            value={templateId}
            disabled={loadingTemplates || templates.length === 0}
            onChange={(event) => setTemplateId(event.target.value)}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500 disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loadingTemplates && (
              <option value="">Loading announcement types…</option>
            )}
            {!loadingTemplates && templates.length === 0 && (
              <option value="">No announcement types available</option>
            )}
            {templates.map((template) => (
              <option key={template.template_id} value={template.template_id}>
                {template.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {selectedTemplate && (
            <div className="mt-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 p-4">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="rounded-full bg-fuchsia-500/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-fuchsia-300">
                  {selectedTemplate.category}
                </span>
                <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                  Approved version {selectedTemplate.version}
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-400">
                {selectedTemplate.description}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          <label
            htmlFor="announcement-title"
            className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
          >
            Publication title
          </label>
          <input
            id="announcement-title"
            value={title}
            onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
            className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
            placeholder="Announcement title"
          />

          {selectedTemplate?.fields.map((field) => (
            <div key={field.name}>
              <label
                htmlFor={`template-field-${field.name}`}
                className="mt-5 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500"
              >
                {field.label}
                {field.required && (
                  <span className="ml-1 text-fuchsia-300">*</span>
                )}
              </label>

              {field.multiline ? (
                <textarea
                  id={`template-field-${field.name}`}
                  value={values[field.name] ?? ""}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateValue(field.name, event.target.value)
                  }
                  rows={5}
                  className="mt-2 w-full resize-y rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
                />
              ) : (
                <input
                  id={`template-field-${field.name}`}
                  value={values[field.name] ?? ""}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateValue(field.name, event.target.value)
                  }
                  className="mt-2 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-white outline-none focus:border-fuchsia-500"
                />
              )}
            </div>
          ))}

          {selectedTemplate && requiredFieldsMissing && (
            <p className="mt-4 text-xs text-amber-300">
              Complete all required fields before previewing or saving.
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={!canPreview || previewing}
              onClick={handlePreview}
              className="rounded-xl border border-fuchsia-500/50 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-fuchsia-200 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              {previewing ? "Previewing…" : "Preview"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              disabled={!canPreview || saving}
              onClick={handleSaveDraft}
              className="rounded-xl bg-fuchsia-500 px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40"
            >
              {saving ? "Saving…" : "Save draft"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/70 p-6">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-fuchsia-300">
            Preview
          </p>

          <div className="mt-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-800 bg-[#050816] p-6">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">
              EasyChatBot Academy
            </p>
            <h2 className="mt-3 text-2xl font-bold text-white">
              {title || "Announcement title"}
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-4 whitespace-pre-wrap text-sm leading-7 text-slate-300">
              {preview ||
                "Complete the selected announcement type and choose Preview to see the rendered announcement."}
            </p>
          </div>
        </section>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

####################################################################
FILE: frontend/app/lib/api/announcementTemplateEditor.ts
####################################################################
export interface TemplateEditorField {
  name: string;
  required: boolean;
  label: string;
  multiline: boolean;
}

export interface TemplateEditorOption {
  template_id: string;
  name: string;
  category: string;
  description: string;
  version: number;
  publication_type: string;
  fields: TemplateEditorField[];
}

export interface TemplateEditorPreview {
  template_id: string;
  template_version: number;
  publication_type: string;
  content: string;
}

export interface CreatedDraftPublication {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  content: string;
  template_id: string;
  template_version: number;
  publication_type: string;
  status: string;
  created_at: string | null;
}

const API_BASE =
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "http://localhost:8000";

async function request<T>(
  path: string,
  init?: RequestInit,
): Promise<T> {
  const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: {
      Accept: "application/json",
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      ...(init?.headers ?? {}),
    },
    cache: "no-store",
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    let detail = `Announcement template API returned ${response.status}.`;

    try {
      const body = (await response.json()) as { detail?: string };
      if (body.detail) {
        detail = body.detail;
      }
    } catch {
      // Preserve the stable HTTP error for non-JSON responses.
    }

    throw new Error(detail);
  }

  return response.json() as Promise<T>;
}

export function getEditorTemplates(): Promise<TemplateEditorOption[]> {
  return request<TemplateEditorOption[]>(
    "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/templates",
  );
}

export function previewTemplate(input: {
  templateId: string;
  templateVersion: number;
  values: Record<string, string>;
}): Promise<TemplateEditorPreview> {
  return request<TemplateEditorPreview>(
    "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/preview",
    {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        template_id: input.templateId,
        template_version: input.templateVersion,
        values: input.values,
      }),
    },
  );
}

export function createTemplateDraft(input: {
  templateId: string;
  templateVersion: number;
  title: string;
  values: Record<string, string>;
}): Promise<CreatedDraftPublication> {
  return request<CreatedDraftPublication>(
    "/api/announcement-operations/template-editor/drafts",
    {
      method: "POST",
      body: JSON.stringify({
        template_id: input.templateId,
        template_version: input.templateVersion,
        title: input.title,
        values: input.values,
      }),
    },
  );
}

3. AUTHENTICATED PRINCIPAL DEPENDENCY
====================================================================
PATH: .\backend\app\api\dependencies\identity.py

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, status

from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal
from app.infrastructure.auth.session_identity_provider import (
    SessionIdentityProvider,
)


async def get_current_principal(request: Request) -> Principal:
    """Resolve identity from the HttpOnly application session cookie."""

    session_id = request.cookies.get("easychatbot_session")

    repository = request.app.state.auth_repository

    provider = SessionIdentityProvider(
        session_id=session_id,
        repository=repository,
    )

    principal = await provider.get_current_principal()

    if principal is None or not principal.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Authentication required.",
        )

    return principal

4. CANONICAL AUTHORIZATION DEPENDENCY
====================================================================
PATH: .\backend\app\api\dependencies\authorization.py

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status

from app.api.dependencies.identity import get_current_principal
from app.application.authorization.authorization_service import (
    AuthorizationService,
    PermissionDeniedError,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


def require_permission(permission: Permission):
    async def dependency(
        principal: Principal = Depends(get_current_principal),
    ) -> Principal:
        try:
            return AuthorizationService().require_permission(
                principal=principal,
                permission=permission,
            )
        except PermissionDeniedError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

    return dependency


====================================================================
5. AUTHORIZATION SERVICE
====================================================================
PATH: .\backend\app\application\authorization\authorization_service.py

from __future__ import annotations

from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission, has_permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


class AuthenticationRequiredError(PermissionError):
    """No valid authenticated principal exists."""


class PermissionDeniedError(PermissionError):
    """Authenticated principal lacks a permission."""


class AuthorizationService:
    """Provider-neutral authorization boundary."""

    def require_authenticated(
        self,
        principal: Principal | None,
    ) -> Principal:
        if principal is None or not principal.is_active:
            raise AuthenticationRequiredError(
                "Authenticated application identity is required."
            )
        return principal

    def require_permission(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal | None,
        permission: Permission,
    ) -> Principal:
        authenticated = self.require_authenticated(principal)

        if not has_permission(
            roles=authenticated.roles,
            permission=permission,
        ):
            raise PermissionDeniedError(
                f"Permission required: {permission.value}."
            )

        return authenticated






====================================================================
6. ANNOUNCEMENT PERMISSIONS
====================================================================
PATH: .\backend\app\domain\authorization\permissions.py

from __future__ import annotations

from enum import StrEnum


class Permission(StrEnum):
    ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW = "announcement.view"
    ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH = "announcement.publish"
    FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL = "functionality.control"
    INTERNAL_DATA_READ = "internal_data.read"


class Role(StrEnum):
    BETA_USER = "beta_user"
    OPERATOR = "operator"
    ADMIN = "admin"


ROLE_PERMISSIONS: dict[Role, frozenset[Permission]] = {
    Role.BETA_USER: frozenset(),
    Role.OPERATOR: frozenset(
        {
            Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW,
            Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH,
            Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL,
            Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
        }
    ),
    Role.ADMIN: frozenset(
        {
            Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_VIEW,
            Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH,
            Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL,
            Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
        }
    ),
}


def has_permission(
    *,
    roles: frozenset[str],
    permission: Permission,
) -> bool:
    return any(
        permission in ROLE_PERMISSIONS.get(
            Role(raw_role),
            frozenset(),
        )
        for raw_role in roles
        if raw_role in {role.value for role in Role}
    )

7. CANONICAL PUBLICATION DOMAIN
====================================================================
PATH: .\backend\app\domain\announcements\publication.py

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from .template_models import PublicationStatus, PublicationType


_ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS = {
    PublicationStatus.DRAFT: {
        PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL,
        PublicationStatus.CANCELLED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL: {
        PublicationStatus.APPROVED,
        PublicationStatus.CANCELLED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.APPROVED: {
        PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING,
        PublicationStatus.CANCELLED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING: {
        PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED,
        PublicationStatus.FAILED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.FAILED: {
        PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING,
        PublicationStatus.CANCELLED,
    },
    PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED: set(),
    PublicationStatus.CANCELLED: set(),
}


@dataclass
class Publication:
    publication_id: str
    template_id: str
    template_version: int
    publication_type: PublicationType
    title: str
    content: str
    status: PublicationStatus = PublicationStatus.DRAFT
    created_at: datetime | None = None
    approved_at: datetime | None = None
    published_at: datetime | None = None
    failure_reason: str | None = None

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.publication_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("publication_id cannot be empty.")
        if not self.template_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("template_id cannot be empty.")
        if self.template_version < 1:
            raise ValueError("template_version must be >= 1.")
        if not self.title.strip():
            raise ValueError("title cannot be empty.")
        if not self.content.strip():
            raise ValueError("content cannot be empty.")
        if self.created_at is None:
            self.created_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    def transition(self, target: PublicationStatus) -> None:
        if target not in _ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS[self.status]:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid publication transition: "
                f"{self.status.value} -> {target.value}"
            )
        self.status = target
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if target is PublicationStatus.APPROVED:
            self.approved_at = now
        elif target is PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED:
            self.published_at = now
            self.failure_reason = None

    def request_approval(self) -> None:
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.PENDING_APPROVAL)

    def approve(self) -> None:
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.APPROVED)

    def begin_publish(self) -> None:
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING)

    def mark_failure(self, reason: str) -> None:
        if not reason.strip():
            raise ValueError("failure reason cannot be empty.")
        if self.status is not PublicationStatus.PUBLISHING:
            raise ValueError("A publication can only fail while publishing.")
        self.failure_reason = reason.strip()
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.FAILED)

    def publish_success(self) -> None:
        self.transition(PublicationStatus.PUBLISHED)

9. SQLALCHEMY PUBLICATION REPOSITORY
====================================================================
PATH: .\backend\app\infrastructure\announcements\sqlalchemy_publication_repository.py

"""PostgreSQL adapter for the existing PublicationRepository contract."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, Text, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.application.announcements.repositories import PublicationRepository
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication
from app.domain.announcements.template_models import (
    PublicationStatus,
    PublicationType,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base


class PublicationModel(Base):
    """Canonical durable announcement publication state."""

    __tablename__ = "announcement_publications"

    publication_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), primary_key=True)
    template_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    template_version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    publication_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), nullable=False)
    content: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False, index=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    approved_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    published_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True))
    failure_reason: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text)


class SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository(PublicationRepository):
    """Async PostgreSQL adapter for the existing repository contract."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    def _to_domain(self, model: PublicationModel) -> Publication:
        return Publication(
            publication_id=model.publication_id,
            template_id=model.template_id,
            template_version=model.template_version,
            publication_type=PublicationType(model.publication_type),
            title=model.title,
            content=model.content,
            status=PublicationStatus(model.status),
            created_at=model.created_at,
            approved_at=model.approved_at,
            published_at=model.published_at,
            failure_reason=model.failure_reason,
        )

    def _from_domain(self, publication: Publication) -> PublicationModel:
        return PublicationModel(
            publication_id=publication.publication_id,
            template_id=publication.template_id,
            template_version=publication.template_version,
            publication_type=publication.publication_type.value,
            title=publication.title,
            content=publication.content,
            status=publication.status.value,
            created_at=publication.created_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            approved_at=publication.approved_at,
            published_at=publication.published_at,
            failure_reason=publication.failure_reason,
        )

    async def save_async(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            model = await session.get(
                PublicationModel,
                publication.publication_id,
            )
            if model is None:
                session.add(self._from_domain(publication))
            else:
                model.template_id = publication.template_id
                model.template_version = publication.template_version
                model.publication_type = publication.publication_type.value
                model.title = publication.title
                model.content = publication.content
                model.status = publication.status.value
                model.created_at = publication.created_at or model.created_at
                model.approved_at = publication.approved_at
                model.published_at = publication.published_at
                model.failure_reason = publication.failure_reason
            await session.commit()

    async def get_async(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            model = await session.get(PublicationModel, publication_id)
            return self._to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def list_all_async(self) -> tuple[Publication, ...]:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationModel).order_by(PublicationModel.created_at)
            )
            return tuple(self._to_domain(model) for model in result.scalars().all())

    # The existing repository contract is synchronous. These methods make
    # accidental async DB use explicit rather than silently blocking.
    def save(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use save_async()."
        )

    def get(self, publication_id: str) -> Publication | None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use get_async()."
        )

    def list_all(self) -> tuple[Publication, ...]:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "SqlAlchemyPublicationRepository requires its async boundary; "
            "use list_all_async()."
        )
